TL;DR: 在预算B远小于LLM智能体数量N的审计场景中，本文通过两级高斯copula模型建模预算受限的带噪检查，定位了置信度排序审计劣于随机审计的校准阈值δ*，并发现两个反直觉结论：预算越小δ*越高、跨族相关性并非较低（共享难度主导谱系）。实测五个开源LLM的置信度近乎恒定且无操作价值，而专有模型信息量充足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个人类审计员需要在预算受限（B≪N）下审计大量LLM智能体，而智能体自报的置信度可能被对抗性错误校准并存在相关性误差，这使审计策略的可靠性成为关键问题。

Method: 将问题建模为预算受限的两级高斯copula带噪检查问题，理论推导出置信度排序审计优于随机审计的误校准阈值δ*；用五个开源LLM和一个专有模型进行实证评估，并在记录轨迹上回放策略验证排序。

Result: 预算越小δ*越高（反直觉）；跨族相关性并非低，共享难度主导；五个开源LLM置信度近恒定、操作上无用，点估计达到或超过翻转阈值但置信区间跨过阈值；专有模型置信度有信息量且低于阈值。

Conclusion: 给出了"空洞监督"（vacuous oversight）的定量判据，并通过轨迹回放验证了策略排序，为LLM审计中的置信度使用提供了理论边界与实证指导。

Abstract: A single human must audit $N$ LLM agents under a budget of $B \ll N$ audits per round, guided by self-reported confidence that may be adversarially miscalibrated and by correlated errors. We model this as budgeted noisy inspection over a two-level Gaussian copula and locate the miscalibration threshold $δ^*$ past which confidence-ranked auditing is \emph{worse} than random. Two a-priori expectations reverse: $δ^*$ \emph{rises} as the budget shrinks, and cross-family correlation is not low---shared difficulty dominates lineage. Five open-weight LLMs show operationally useless (near-constant) confidence, point estimates at or beyond the flip though CIs straddle it; a proprietary model is informative and lands below it. We give a quantitative criterion for \emph{vacuous} oversight, and replaying policies on recorded traces confirms the ordering.

</details>


### [166] [PathView-Bench: Can Multimodal Large Language Models Achieve Fine-grained Multiscale Understanding of Pathology Images?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28318)
*Zongyi Chen,Yu Liang,Jie Lin,Liansheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathVU是一个面向病理学多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的视觉锚定基准，包含14个VQA式任务、61,673张图像和308,070个样本，覆盖28个器官和7,253,526个标注，在区域和全切片两个视野下系统评估模型的细粒度多尺度视觉理解能力。对18个代表性MLLMs的评测表明，即便先进的模型在病理图像细粒度视觉任务中仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流病理学多模态基准主要评估最终诊断答案、报告或描述，无法判断模型是否真正理解病理推理和决策所需的多尺度视觉内容。需要一个新的基准来显式评估模型在细粒度、多尺度层面的视觉理解能力。

Method: 从23个公开病理影像数据集中构建PathVU，利用人工监督的标签和空间标注，将原始标注转化为确定性任务目标，实现程序化评分。基准涵盖两个视野（区域高分辨率局部视图和全切片宏观视图），包含区域定位、视觉识别、数量估计、空间推理和上下文不足判断等五类能力维度，共14个VQA式任务。

Result: 对18个代表性MLLMs（涵盖通用、医学领域和病理专项三类）进行评测，发现即使在先进的模型中，在跨多尺度病理图像的细粒度视觉任务上仍存在明显的能力局限。

Conclusion: PathVU为开发和评估病理学MLLMs提供了可复现的基准基础，强调显式多尺度视觉理解的重要性，并揭示了当前模型在此方面的不足。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to analyze pathology images. However, dominant multimodal benchmarks in pathology mainly score final diagnostic answers, captions, or reports. These evaluations provide limited insight into whether a model understands the multiscale visual content needed for pathology reasoning and decision-making. We introduce PathVU, a vision-anchored benchmark for fine-grained and multiscale visual understanding in computational pathology. Built from 23 public pathology imaging datasets with human-supervised labels and spatial annotations, PathVU evaluates MLLM understanding in two fields of view: Region FOV for high-resolution local regions and Slide FOV for macro whole-slide views. By converting raw annotations into deterministic task targets, PathVU enables programmatic scoring of region localization, visual recognition, quantity estimation, spatial reasoning, and insufficient-context judgment. The benchmark contains 14 VQA-style tasks, 61,673 images, and 308,070 samples across 28 organs and 7,253,526 annotations. Evaluating 18 representative general-purpose, medical-domain, and pathology-oriented MLLMs, we observe substantial limitations even in advanced models on fine-grained visual tasks across multiscale pathology images. PathVU provides a reproducible basis for developing and evaluating pathology MLLMs with explicit multiscale visual understanding.

</details>


### [167] [Paying for Honesty Without Knowing the Truth: Reputation-Penalty Design for LLM Marketplace Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28330)
*Mingdai Yang,Shicheng Fan,Kejing Yu,Duohao Wang,Li Sun,Hao Peng,Philip S. Yu,Zhiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARP（带死区的声誉惩罚机制）和SPARC（代码门控反思机制），解决LLM代理在电商场景中的商品属性捏造问题。CARP利用带死区的声誉惩罚机制，在没有真实标签的情况下，仅依据有噪声的投诉信号来惩罚低信誉卖家、保护消费者；SPARC则通过字节清洁的代码门控反思机制，让捏造行为因销量损失而变得"行为上可约束"，显著缩小了与完美信息Oracle之间的消费者福利差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理作为自主商家编写商品列表时，即使被指示要诚实，在竞争压力下仍会在大多数列表（跨多个模型）中捏造商品属性。平台显而易见的应对措施——逐条核验真实属性——不可行，因为平台只能观测到有噪声且有偏的投诉信号，永远无法获得真实标签。因此需要一个不依赖产品级真实标签、对投诉噪声鲁棒且能抵抗策略性博弈的机制。

Method: 1) CARP：一种声誉惩罚机制，包含两个关键设计——"死区"（deadband）用于容忍投诉噪声，避免误伤诚实商家；"状态依赖的惩罚强度"（state-dependent severity）用于对抗声誉驱动的检测侵蚀（即高声誉商家更不易被投诉，导致惩罚失效）。该机制不需要产品级真实标签，对策略性博弈鲁棒。2) SPARC：字节清洁的代码门控反思机制，通过让捏造行为直接关联销量损失，使惩罚在行为层面产生约束力（fabrication costs them sales），而非仅靠合规指令约束。通过惩罚门控的自纠正推理（penalty-gated self-correction reasoning）实现。

Result: CARP能够抑制低评级撒谎者的销量、保护消费者，同时不误伤诚实卖家。与SPARC配合使用时，在完全不获取真实标签的情况下，填补了相对于完美信息Oracle的大部分消费者福利差距，在所有对比策略中取得最佳福利表现。惩罚通过SPARC在行为层面产生约束力——LLM商家在"撒谎免费"时捏造，但当捏造造成销量损失时自我克制，这是一种自利反应而非合规行为。该效应跨多个模型一致观察到，并有置信区间支持。

Conclusion: 仅靠诚实指令无法阻止LLM商家捏造商品属性；需要设计将捏造成本内化到商家收益中的机制性解决方案。CARP+SPARC的组合证明，即使平台无法获得真实标签，仍能通过设计良好的声誉惩罚与行为约束机制，显著逼近完美信息下的最优消费者福利，为LLM代理市场的可信度治理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act as autonomous merchants that write their own product listings, and under competitive pressure, they fabricate attributes to win sales. Even under instructions to be honest, they fabricate attributes in a majority of listings across models. A platform's obvious remedy---verifying each claim against the truth---is unavailable, because it observes only a noisy, biased complaint signal, never the ground truth. We design CARP, a reputation-penalty mechanism with a deadband that forgives complaint noise and a state-dependent severity that counters reputation-driven detection erosion. CARP requires no product-level ground truth and is robust to strategic gaming. CARP protects consumers by suppressing the sales volume of low-rated liars while sparing honest sellers. Paired with SPARC, it closes most of the consumer-welfare gap relative to a perfect-information oracle, without ever accessing the truth. It also achieves the best welfare of the policies we compare. We further show that this felt penalty becomes behaviorally binding through SPARC, a byte-clean code-gated reflection mechanism: LLM merchants fabricate when lying is free but restrain themselves when fabrication costs them sales, a self-interested response rather than compliance. We trace this distinction to penalty-gated self-correction reasoning, and observe the binding across models, with supporting confidence intervals.

</details>


### [168] [Correcting What You Cannot See: Credit Assignment for Perception Distillation in Multimodal Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28336)
*Feng Xiong,Leyan Xue,Hongyu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出感知纠正蒸馏（PCD），一种无标签方法，通过下游失败与学生-教师不一致两个互补证据的乘积（软AND门）来识别可纠正的感知失败，从而在多模态推理蒸馏中精准施加监督，而不改变推理目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同策略蒸馏为多模态推理器提供密集监督，但其轨迹级奖励无法区分失败源于感知还是后续推理。现有的感知成功率（PSR）估计存在歧义，因为低成功率混淆了感知不足与推理难度，需要一种能精确识别可纠正感知失败的方法。

Method: PCD使用下游失败和学生-教师不一致作为互补证据，两者的乘积形成软AND门，仅在两个证据同时存在时增强蒸馏。该方法通过贝叶斯证据组合论证乘积规则的合理性，并证明乘法是唯一的归一化双线性门，当任一证据缺失时即归零。PCD采用分离的感知-推理回滚和均值保持权重，保持推理目标不变。

Result: 在八个基准上，PCD将8B→2B的宏平均从44.50（OPD）提升到47.28，32B→8B从56.94提升到61.22。在匹配的2B消融实验中，移除PCD和分离回滚分别使保留集平均分降低2.22和0.88分。

Conclusion: 有效的多模态蒸馏不仅取决于教师预测什么，还取决于识别何时感知才是纠正的合适目标。

Abstract: On-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multimodal reasoners, but its trajectory-level reward cannot determine whether a failed answer arose from perception or subsequent reasoning. Perception Success Rate (PSR), estimated from multiple reasonings sharing one perception, remains ambiguous because low success conflates perceptual insufficiency with reasoning difficulty. We introduce \textbf{Perception-Correction Distillation (PCD)}, a label-free method that identifies correctable perception failures using downstream failure and teacher--student disagreement as complementary witnesses. Their product, , forms a soft AND gate that strengthens distillation only when both witnesses are present. We motivate this rule through Bayesian evidence combination and show that multiplication is the unique normalized bilinear gate that vanishes when either witness is absent. PCD uses separated perception--reasoning rollouts and mean-preserving weights, leaving the reasoning objective unchanged. Across eight benchmarks, PCD improves the 8B 2B macro average from 44.50 with OPD to 47.28 and the 32B 8B result from 56.94 to 61.22. In matched 2B ablations, removing PCD and separated rollout reduces held-out average by 2.22 and 0.88 points, respectively. Effective multimodal distillation therefore depends not only on what the teacher predicts, but also on identifying when perception is the appropriate target of correction.

</details>


### [169] [How Benchmarks Mis-Score Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28367)
*Zihan Dong,Zhiyuan Ma,Zekun Wang,Yunqing Li,Zirou Liu,Ruixuan Deng,Qishi Zhan,Rui Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地审视了计算机使用智能体（CUA）基准评测的可靠性问题，将整个评测流水线划分为任务构建、轨迹观测、评分和报告四个环节，通过审计五大基准中150条公开失败轨迹，发现15.3%的FAIL判定是错误的，并将真实失败归类为三层次诊断分类体系。最后，作者将发现与更新的长时程CUA基准关联，提出分阶段的设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）正被广泛部署用于浏览网页和操作桌面软件，但其基准分数仍常由脆弱的脚本化评估器生成。现有评测流程中任务可能过时、轨迹可能缺少关键视觉证据、评估器可能拒绝正确替代方案、聚合报告可能掩盖失败原因，这些问题导致评测结果可靠性存疑，亟需系统性的可靠性框架来识别和修正。

Method: 作者首先构建了一个覆盖四个环节（任务构建、轨迹观测、评分、报告）的可靠性框架。随后对来自五个代表性基准（涵盖网页、企业工作流和桌面控制）的150条公开失败轨迹进行人工审计，量化评估错误，并提出一个三层次诊断分类体系（tiered taxonomy）对真实失败进行分类归因。

Result: 审计结果显示：15.3%的FAIL判定是错误的，其中包括10.7%的评估器假阴性（正确轨迹被误判为失败）和4.7%的任务本身损坏。对真实失败而言，三层次诊断分类表明验证/反馈错误和规划错误占据主导地位，而执行/基础（grounding）错误相对较少，且单一的标量成功率无法解释这些失败模式的差异。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出当前CUA基准评测存在系统性可靠性缺陷，单一成功率指标不足以反映失败模式的结构性差异。作者将发现与更长时间域（long-horizon）的新兴CUA基准相联系，并据此推导出针对CUA评测流程各阶段的设计规则，为构建更可靠的CUA评估体系提供了指导。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUA) are being deployed to browse the web and operate desktop software, yet their benchmark scores are still commonly produced by brittle scripted oracles. A score is the output of a pipeline in which tasks can be stale, trajectories can omit decisive visual evidence, evaluators can reject valid alternatives, and aggregate reports can hide the cause of failure. We organize these problems into a reliability framework spanning task construction, trajectory observation, scoring, and reporting. We then audit 150 public failure-scored trajectories from five web, enterprise-workflow, and desktop-control benchmarks, find that 15.3\% of FAIL verdicts are wrong: 10.7\% are evaluator false negatives and 4.7\% are broken tasks. For genuine failures, a three-tier diagnostic taxonomy shows that verification/feedback and planning failures dominate execution/grounding errors, while a single scalar success rate can not explain. We connect these findings to newer long-horizon CUA benchmarks and derive stage-specific design rules for CUA evaluation.

</details>


### [170] [HyperClaim: Fine-Grained Cross-Modal Hypergraph Reasoning for Video Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28375)
*Xiangbo Wang,Jiasheng Zhang,Xingtong Yu,Luoqiang Lei,Delvin Ce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HyperClaim，一个基于判别式时序超图框架的视频虚假信息检测方法，通过构建稀疏异质超图捕获查询文本、证据文本与视频帧之间的高阶跨模态交互，在三个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频虚假信息检测方法（全局多模态融合或自由形式多模态推理）都会丢失局部真实性线索，这些线索源于查询短语、上下文文本和短视频片段之间的耦合交互。此类交互本质上是高阶的，成对图模型无法捕获多路跨模态依赖，而超图能自然表征这种关系。

Method: HyperClaim包含四个核心模块：(1)以标题或基准配对文本作为claim查询，构建覆盖查询token、证据token和采样帧的稀疏异质超图；(2)采用置信度感知过滤和源预算策略，形成紧凑的文本-帧及短时序证据单元；(3)进行自适应软关联推理并结合残差文本-视频校准；(4)通过差异感知readout聚合文本、视觉和超边状态。

Result: 在FactGuard时序协议下，FakeSV、FakeTT和FakeVV上分别达到83.7%、82.0%和87.3%的准确率，超越强判别式和推理中心基线。学习到的关联与注意力权重揭示token级和帧级结构。

Conclusion: HyperClaim无需生成rationale或外部工具调用，保留了全局融合容易压平的细粒度跨模态与时序结构，在多个基准上验证了超图建模对视频虚假信息检测的有效性。

Abstract: Video misinformation detection is often approached through global multimodal fusion or free-form multimodal reasoning. Both paradigms can under-represent localized authenticity cues that arise from coupled interactions among query phrases, contextual text, and short temporal spans of frames. Because such interactions are inherently higher-order, pairwise graph formulations are insufficient to capture multi-way cross-modal dependencies, whereas hypergraphs offer a suitable representation for these relations. We propose HyperClaim, a discriminative temporal hypergraph framework for sample-level authenticity classification. Using the title or benchmark-provided paired text as a claim-like query, HyperClaim constructs a sparse heterogeneous hypergraph over query tokens, evidence tokens, and sampled frames; applies confidence-aware filtering and source budgeting to form compact text-frame and short-range temporal evidence units; performs adaptive soft-incidence reasoning with residual text-video calibration; and aggregates textual, visual, and hyperedge states through a discrepancy-aware readout. Without relying on generated rationales or external tool calls, HyperClaim preserves fine-grained cross-modal and temporal structure that global fusion tends to flatten. Under the FactGuard temporal protocol, it achieves 83.7%, 82.0%, and 87.3% accuracy on FakeSV, FakeTT, and FakeVV, respectively, outperforming strong discriminative and reasoning-centric baselines. Learned incidence and attention weights further reveal token- and frame-level structure.

</details>


### [171] [When Specifications Conflict: A Symmetry-Based Framework for Measuring LLM Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28384)
*Tairan Wang,Liang Zhou,Zikang Zhan,Pingchuan Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个受控实验框架，用于研究大语言模型在相互冲突的规格说明之间如何做出偏好选择。框架基于对称性设计减少混淆因素，并在数学基准、布尔代数、代码生成和临床领域等多个任务上验证，发现模型表现出系统性的偏好排序：形式语言≈自然化形式语言>纯自然语言>输入-输出示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地需要整合可能不一致或冲突的多源信息，但目前缺乏可控且可归因的方法来分析模型如何在竞争性规格之间解决冲突。

Method: 提出一个受控实验框架：通过构造具有显式冲突的规格对让模型在竞争规格间做出选择，从而直接观察和分析模型偏好；采用对称性设计减少混淆因素，使不同表示类型间的偏好可系统比较。在包含550个冲突实例、覆盖11个函数族别的可执行数学基准上评估，比较四种表示类型：纯自然语言、形式语言、自然化形式语言、输入-输出示例；并扩展到异构规格冲突场景（布尔代数、代码生成、临床领域）。

Result: 模型表现出系统性偏好模式而非随机行为，一致性排序为：形式语言≈自然化形式语言>纯自然语言>输入-输出示例。示例效应进一步取决于模型能力和函数族别。框架在异构规格冲突场景（布尔代数、代码生成、临床领域）中同样适用。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种统一的方法来度量LLM如何解决竞争性信息来源之间的冲突，具有跨多种任务和规格形式的广泛适用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly required to integrate multiple sources of information that may be inconsistent or conflicting. However, there is still a lack of controllable and attributable methods for analyzing how models resolve conflicts between competing specifications. We propose a controlled experimental framework for studying model preferences under conflicting specifications. By constructing specifications with explicit conflicts, the framework enables model choices between competing specifications to be directly observed and analyzed. A symmetry-based design further reduces confounding factors, allowing preferences across representation types to be compared systematically.
  We evaluate the framework on an executable mathematical benchmark with 550 conflict instances spanning 11 function families, comparing four representation types: pure natural language, formal language, naturalized formal language, and input--output examples. Results show systematic preference patterns rather than random behavior, with a consistent ordering: $ \text{Formal} \approx \text{Naturalized Formal} > \text{Pure Natural Language} > \text{Input--Output Examples} $. Example effects further depend on model capability and function family.
  We extend the framework to heterogeneous specification conflicts in Boolean algebra, code generation, and the clinical domain, demonstrating its applicability across diverse tasks and specification forms. The framework provides a unified approach for measuring how LLMs resolve conflicts between competing sources of information.

</details>


### [172] [QuantWAMs: Calibrating at the Right Granularity for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28405)
*Jiacheng Zhou,Jinfan Lv,Ruixuan Li,Longtai Zhang,Yan Wang,Wenqiang Zhang,Lizhe Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuantWAMs是一个专门针对世界动作模型（WAMs）的后训练量化（PTQ）框架，通过三种策略（共享基异常校准、协同训练目标显著性、固定干预滚动审计）将量化决策与模型结构、部署分布和任务目标对齐。在Fast-WAM和LingBot-VA上，W4A4条件下量化后与FP16的仿真指标仅差0.2–0.7个百分点，峰值权重与激活内存降至FP16的约29%，并提供1.4–1.6倍块级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）联合预测未来观测与动作，但其迭代去噪与闭环执行特性使得高效部署成本高昂。现有PTQ方法不适用于WAMs，因为它们依赖开环目标、同质模型假设以及不能反映实际部署场景的校准分布。

Method: QuantWAMs提出三种策略：(1) 共享基异常校准，仅在坐标兼容模块间池化激活证据；(2) 协同训练目标显著性，从联合视频-动作梯度计算经验Fisher分数，在校准稳定的层粒度上分配权重精度；(3) 固定干预滚动审计，在不改变精度预算的情况下，利用可达的闭环状态修订去噪步保护调度。

Result: 在Fast-WAM和LingBot-VA上，覆盖RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及AgiBot G2真实机器人操作实验。W4A4主导设置下，仿真均值与FP16相差0.2–0.7个百分点；真实机器人实验中三个操作任务验证部署可行性；目标视频与动作块的峰值权重和激活内存降至FP16的约29%，块级加速1.4–1.6倍。

Conclusion: QuantWAMs通过将量化决策与部署校准上下文（模型结构、滚动分布和任务目标）对齐，有效解决了WAMs后训练量化的挑战，在保持接近FP16精度的同时显著降低内存并实现加速，验证了其在真实机器人操作任务中的部署可行性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future observations and actions, but their iterative denoising and closed-loop execution make efficient deployment costly. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods are poorly suited to WAMs because they rely on open-loop objectives, homogeneous model assumptions, and calibration distributions that do not reflect deployment. We present QuantWAMs, a PTQ framework that aligns quantization decisions with the calibration context defined by model structure, rollout distribution, and task objective. QuantWAMs introduces three strategies: shared-basis outlier calibration, which pools activation evidence only across coordinate-compatible modules; co-training-objective saliency, which computes empirical-Fisher scores from the joint video--action gradient and assigns weight precision at a calibration-stable layer granularity; and fixed-intervention rollout auditing, which revises denoising-step protection schedules using reachable closed-loop states without changing the precision budget. We evaluate QuantWAMs on Fast-WAM and LingBot-VA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and real-robot manipulation with an AgiBot G2. Under a W4A4-dominant setting, the reported simulation means differ from FP16 by 0.2--0.7 percentage points. Real-robot trials further establish deployment feasibility on three manipulation tasks. For the targeted video and action blocks, QuantWAMs reduces peak weight-and-activation memory to about 29\% of FP16 and provides 1.4--1.6$\times$ block-level speedups.

</details>


### [173] [When Derived Measurements Mislead: Quantifying and Mitigating LLM Over-Trust with Privileged-Modality Reliability Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28421)
*Zongheng Guo,Tao Chen,Tianli Li,Mingzhe Cui,Yang Jiao,Lei Xie,Yi Pan,Xiao Hu,Manuela Ferrario*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了派生特征过度信任（DFOT）问题，即LLM将派生测量（如PPG信号衍生的心电节律）错误地当作直接事实或超出其有效范围使用。以生理传感为案例，提出五个评估指标（COTR、CIR、CRR、ESRM、UHR），并在50,000对PPG-ECG记录上验证了基于ECG-to-PPG特权蒸馏的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着派生测量越来越多地以直接事实的形式进入LLM流水线，其有效性与实例相关，可能导致下游LLM过度信任并错误使用这些派生特征。现有研究缺乏对这一系统性失败模式的统一度量和评估框架。

Method: 定义DFOT概念，以PPG-ECG生理传感为案例设计两个测试任务（D1接受被ECG反驳的PPG派生节律，D2拒绝离线确认可靠的PPG节律）；提出五个估计算量：COTR（冲突过度信任率）、CIR（情境诱导错误率）、CRR（修正修复率）、ESRM（证据特异性修复裕度）、UHR（不必要验证率）；采用ECG-to-PPG特权蒸馏作为基线方法。

Result: 在协议锁定的187名患者测试集上，基线方法在四个修复和特异性端点上的改进为1.82-6.69个百分点，所有配对置信区间均排除零；UHR增加0.67个百分点（95% CI: -0.4至+1.7）。

Conclusion: DFOT提供了一个统一的评估目标，可用于比较和推动更强的派生测量误导缓解方法的发展。

Abstract: Derived measurements increasingly enter large language model (LLM) pipelines as direct facts despite their instance-dependent validity. We define derived-feature over-trust (DFOT) as the failure in which a downstream LLM assigns such a measurement the epistemic status of a direct fact or uses it outside its valid scope. Using physiological sensing as a case study, D1 tests acceptance of a PPG-derived rhythm contradicted by offline ECG, whereas D2 tests rejection of an offline-confirmed reliable PPG rhythm under misleading severe history. ECG supplies training supervision and offline reference construction but is never shown to the LLM. Five estimands quantify this chain: conflict over-trust rate (COTR) and context-induced error rate (CIR) characterize D1/D2; correct repair rate (CRR) measures frozen-error repair; evidence-specific repair margin (ESRM) contrasts matched and patient-disjoint shuffled evidence; and utility harm rate (UHR) measures unnecessary verification among HIGH-reliability cases used without verification at baseline. The framework does not depend on a particular reliability generator. We demonstrate it on 50,000 paired PPG-ECG records using ECG-to-PPG privileged distillation as an illustrative baseline and PPG-only inference. On a protocol-locked 187-patient test, the baseline improves four repair and specificity endpoints by 1.82-6.69 percentage points, with all paired confidence intervals excluding zero; UHR increases by 0.67 percentage points (95% CI: -0.4 to +1.7). DFOT provides a common evaluation target for stronger mitigation methods. The code is available at https://github.com/Zongheng-Guo/When-Derived-Measurements-Mislead.

</details>


### [174] [A foundation model of numerical intelligence with cross-disciplinary generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28432)
*Chenghan Wu,Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UNICON（统一上下文算子网络），一种展现"数值智能"的基础模型。它通过从同一系统中基于图结构的示例作为上下文，推断系统间共享的预测关系并应用于新查询，无需重训练即可跨学科（包括训练中未见过的学科）逼近专家性能。与语言模型代理结合后，甚至能超越训练中未见学科的最先进专家模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型展示了从文本上下文获取和应用知识的能力，但智能不应局限于语言。科学和社会系统需要从数值上下文中获取和应用知识的模型，即"数值智能"。现有模型缺乏这种跨学科、无需重训练即可泛化的数值推理能力。

Method: UNICON采用基于图的示例作为上下文，从同一系统中推断共享的预测关系，并将其应用于来自该系统的查询。该方法支持上下文学习式的数值推理，并可组合语言模型代理进一步提升性能。

Result: UNICON跨科学和社会系统均逼近专家性能，包括训练中未见的学科；结合语言模型代理后超越训练中未见学科的最先进专家；训练语料多样性可提升对未见学科的泛化能力。

Conclusion: UNICON确立了作为数值智能基础模型的地位，可作为更广泛人工智能生态系统的构建模块，扩展了智能的定义和应用边界。

Abstract: Intelligence is commonly understood as the ability to acquire and apply knowledge, adapt to unfamiliar situations and solve new problems. Large language models exhibit this capacity by inferring task-relevant knowledge from textual context and applying it to new tasks. Yet intelligence need not be confined to language. For scientific and social systems, we need models that acquire and apply knowledge from numerical context-an ability we call numerical intelligence. Here we introduce UNified In-Context Operator Networks (UNICON), a foundation model that exhibits numerical intelligence across disciplines. Using graph-based examples from a system as context, UNICON infers the predictive relation shared across them and applies it to queries from the same system. Across scientific and social systems, including those from disciplines absent from training, the same model approaches specialist performance without retraining. Combining UNICON with language-model agents yields further gains, enabling it to surpass state-of-the-art specialists in a discipline unseen in training. We further show that training-corpus diversity improves generalization to unseen disciplines. Together, these results establish UNICON as a foundation model of numerical intelligence and position it as a building block for a broader ecosystem of artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [175] [LeanCSP: A Framework for Certifying Constraint Reformulation and Solving in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28459)
*Pablo Manrique,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在Lean定理证明器中引入了约束编程验证框架，同时覆盖两个验证层面：公式层面的语义保持性质（等价性、可满足等价性、对称性破缺正确性）以参数化方式针对整个问题族证明；以及通过转换后端的求解器证书检查（MiniZinc、SMT-LIB、OPB）。两者结合形成端到端工作流，无需信任外部求解器即可确认约束问题的可满足性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束编程是解决调度、规划、配置和验证中复杂组合问题的核心技术，但信任其结果需要双重保证：前置改写需保持语义，求解器需给出正确答复。现有方法通常分别处理这两个验证层面，且缺乏参数化证明整个问题族性质的机制。

Method: 在Lean定理证明器中构建统一框架，支持对公式级性质（等价性、可满足等价性、对称性破缺正确性）进行参数化证明，适用于整个问题族的所有实例规模；同时实现证书检查功能，通过翻译后端将实例导出至MiniZinc、SMT-LIB和OPB等外部格式，验证求解器产生的证书。

Result: 实验表明，框架中经验证的对称性破缺实际效果显著：每个问题族只需一次参数化证明即可复用于所有实例规模，将求解器搜索工作量最多降低2×10^7倍；同时整个Lean内证书验证保持可负担开销，最大实例也仅需几分钟。

Conclusion: 该框架为约束编程提供了无需信任外部求解器的端到端端到端验证方案，兼顾公式级性质证明的泛化性（参数化覆盖问题族）与实例级证书检查的实用性，且验证开销在可接受范围内。

Abstract: Constraint programming is a core technology for solving complex combinatorial problems in scheduling, planning, configuration, and verification. Trusting its results therefore demands guarantees at two levels: that reformulations applied beforehand are semantics-preserving, and that solvers produce correct answers. In this work, we introduce a framework that addresses both verification levels in the Lean theorem prover: it can be used to prove formulation-level properties, such as equivalence, equisatisfiability, and the correctness of symmetry-breaking constraints, parametrically for entire problem families; and to check solver-produced certificates for individual instances via translation backends to external formats such as MiniZinc, SMT-LIB, and OPB. Combining both levels yields an end-to-end workflow that establishes the satisfiability or unsatisfiability of a constraint problem without trusting the external solver. Experimental results show that our framework's verified symmetry breaking also pays off in practice: a single parametric proof per problem family, reused across all instance sizes, reduces solver search effort by a factor of up to 2x10^7, while the entire in-Lean certification stays affordable, taking at most a few minutes for our largest instances.

</details>


### [176] [A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28466)
*Jia Yu,Yan Zhu,Yili He,Zilong Wang,Xinyang Jiang,Peiyao Fu,Ruijie Yang,Tianyi Chen,Siyuan Li,Zhihua Wang,Fei Wu,Quanlin Li,Xian Yang,Pinghong Zhou,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoCLIP，一个基于125,756个病灶级图像-文本对训练的结肠镜视觉语言基础模型，从280,476份常规结肠镜报告中渐进恢复数据。在病灶级图文检索、结构化报告生成和六个多中心临床分类任务中，EndoCLIP在零样本和线性探针设置下均优于通用及生物医学视觉语言编码器。在良恶性分类中，其线性探针在12名内镜医师参与的盲法研究中接近专家水平，表明恢复发现-图像对应关系可将常规记录转化为可扩展的监督信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结肠镜报告中包含丰富的专家描述（病变外观、大小、位置），但这些报告概括整个手术过程而非逐帧标注，导致临床发现与对应图像之间的关联很弱。视觉语言模型在结肠镜领域未被充分利用，缺乏大规模、细粒度的图像-文本配对数据是核心瓶颈。

Method: 从280,476份常规结肠镜记录中渐进恢复出125,756个病灶级图像-文本对，用于训练EndoCLIP这一结肠镜视觉语言基础模型。该模型在病灶级图文检索、结构化报告生成以及六个多中心临床分类任务上进行评估，采用零样本和线性探针两种设置。

Result: EndoCLIP在所有评估任务中均优于通用和生物医学视觉语言编码器。在良恶性分类任务中，其线性探针性能接近12名内镜医师参与盲法研究的专家水平。

Conclusion: 恢复发现与图像帧之间的对应关系可以将常规临床文档转化为可扩展的监督来源，使临床目标能够以语言形式指定而无需为每个任务单独标注，从而推动视觉语言模型在结肠镜及其他内镜领域的应用。

Abstract: Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

</details>


### [177] [Towards Autonomous Aircraft Surveillance from Nanosatellites through On-Board Inference and Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28470)
*Antonio Delgado-Rosa,David Muñoz-Valero,Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,Juan Moreno-Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合星上推理与生成式数据增强的工作流，解决纳米卫星下传带宽受限和航空目标数据集类别极度不平衡的双重瓶颈。在搭载低功耗边缘张量加速器的6U立方星上执行推理，用低秩适配微调的扩散模型生成合成少数类图像并通过自动伪标注并入训练集。结果表明，平衡后的数据集使全局mAP从77.9%提升至82.2%，少数类F1从0.683升至0.811，量化检测器适配片上内存并实现星上25-30帧/秒的推理速度，可作为纳米卫星实时自主航空监视的决策支持工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近地轨道航空监视面临两个相互关联的瓶颈：一是纳米卫星下行链路带宽有限，但传统架构仍将海量原始图像传输到地面处理，导致决策延迟；二是公开的航空卫星数据集稀缺且类别严重不平衡，使标准检测器难以学习稀有飞机的稳健表征。二者共同制约了实时、自主的星上航空监视能力。

Method: 提出联合解决两个瓶颈的工作流：(1) 在搭载低功耗边缘张量加速器的6U CubeSat上执行星上推理，避免将全部原始数据下行传输；(2) 采用低秩适配（LoRA）微调的扩散模型生成合成少数类航空影像，通过中间检测器进行自动伪标注，并与经典数据增强样本合并，构建类别平衡的训练集。检测器经量化处理后适配星上芯片内存。

Result: 平衡后的数据集使全局平均精度（mAP）从77.9%提升至82.2%，少数类F1分数从0.683提升至0.811；量化后的检测器能够适配片上内存，并在轨实现25-30帧/秒的推理吞吐量，验证了端到端工作流的可行性。

Conclusion: 计算实验表明，所提出的星上推理与生成式数据增强协同工作流有效克服了带宽限制与数据不平衡两大瓶颈，相较于传统弯管架构（卫星仅作为被动数据收集器），该方案可作为纳米卫星实时、自主航空监视的决策支持工具，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Airborne surveillance from low Earth orbit is hindered by two interconnected bottlenecks: nanosatellites have a limited downlink budget, yet the conventional approach still transmits terabytes of raw imagery to the ground for processing, and open satellite datasets for aircraft are scarce and severely class-imbalanced. These limitations either delay timely decision-making or prevent standard detectors from learning robust representations of rare aircraft classes. In this paper, a workflow that combines on-board inference with generative data augmentation is proposed to address both limitations jointly. Inference is executed on a 6U CubeSat equipped with a low-power edge tensor accelerator, while a diffusion model fine-tuned through low-rank adaptation generates synthetic minority-class imagery. This synthetic output is automatically annotated, pseudo-labelled, by an intermediate detector and merged with classically augmented samples. The results show that the balanced dataset increases global mean average precision from 77.9% to 82.2%, with the minority class rising from F1=0.683 to F1=0.811, and that the quantised detector fits the on-chip memory and projects 25-30 frames per second on orbit. This approach contrasts with the conventional bent-pipe architecture, in which the satellite acts as a passive data collector. Therefore, the computational tests support the proposed workflow as a decision-support tool for real-time, autonomous airborne surveillance from nanosatellites.

</details>


### [178] [A Fuzzy Rule-based Neuro-Symbolic Approach for Pipe Severity Prediction in Sewer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28481)
*Ngoc Thai Le,Thanh Ma,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化的模糊规则神经符号框架，用于自动化污水管道严重程度评估。该框架将Swin Transformer视觉感知模块与决策树（J48）转换而来的19条固定IF-THEN模糊规则相结合，解耦了神经感知与符号推理过程，在3,244张图像的五个高度不平衡严重程度类别数据集上，相比纯图像分类在准确率、平衡准确率、Macro F1和MCC上分别提升了17.9%、12.2%、23.0%和17.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的污水管道严重程度自动评估依赖于直接的图像分类，形成"黑箱"模型，视觉缺陷与最终严重程度分数之间的关联是隐性的、不可解释的。这种缺乏可追溯推理能力的评估方式难以满足工程应用中对可靠性和可解释性的需求。作者旨在构建一个能够解耦神经感知与符号推理的框架，既保持竞争性的分类性能，又提供从视觉缺陷到严重程度评估的可追溯推理路径。

Method: 框架由两个解耦模块组成：(1) 感知模块：使用Swin Transformer从图像直接预测14个多标签检测CODE等级；(2) 推理模块：在真实CODE和严重程度标签上训练决策树（Weka J48），将其路径转换为19条固定的IF-THEN规则。推理阶段采用模糊逻辑，使用t-norm聚合CODE条件激活值，按规则置信度加权后与对应s-norm结合产生可解释的类别证据。评估了Product、Łukasiewicz和Hamacher三种算子对。真实标签通过五个独立大语言模型对原始检查员笔记进行共识生成，数据集包含3,244张图像、五个高度不平衡的严重程度类别。

Result: 在3,244张图像的五个高度不平衡严重程度类别数据集上，该框架相比纯图像分类在准确率、平衡准确率、Macro F1和MCC上分别提升了17.9%、12.2%、23.0%和17.3%。框架在保持竞争性类别平衡性能的同时，实现了从预测CODE等级到规则支持再到严重程度证据的可追溯推理链。

Conclusion: 该神经符号框架成功弥合了视觉缺陷与严重程度评估之间的隐性"黑箱"鸿沟，通过模糊规则实现可解释的推理过程，同时凭借模块化解耦设计在高度不平衡数据上取得优于纯图像分类的性能，为污水管道自动化评估提供了兼具准确性与可追溯性的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard automated sewer pipe severity assessment relies on direct image classification, creating a "black box" where the link between visual defects and final severity scores remains implicit. This study introduces a modular, fuzzy rule-based neuro-symbolic framework that bridges this gap by decoupling neural perception from symbolic reasoning. The perception module utilizes a Swin Transformer to predict 14 multilabel inspection CODE degrees directly from images. For reasoning, a DT, specifically Weka's J48, algorithm is trained on ground-truth CODEs and severity labels, and its paths are converted into 19 fixed IF--THEN rules. Inference operates via fuzzy logic: t-norm activations from CODE conditions are weighted by rule confidence and combined with corresponding s-norms to produce interpretable class evidence. We assessed Product, Łukasiewicz, and Hamacher operator pairs using a dataset of 3,244 images spanning five highly imbalanced severity classes. Ground-truth labels were robustly generated via consensus from five independent large language models analyzing original inspector notes. Our results show an improvement of accuracy, balanced accuracy, Macro F1 and MCC by 17.9%, 12.2%, 23.0%, and 17.3%, respectively, over image-only based classification.
  Overall, the framework combines competitive class-balanced performance with traceable reasoning from predicted CODE degrees to rule supports and severity evidence.

</details>


### [179] [SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28488)
*Yunhao Liang,Xianqi Cao,Pujun Zhang,Yuan Qu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，将供应链AI从孤立的决策模块提升为统一的端到端运营规划。SCOPE将供应链实体表示为token，通过共享运营表征进行上下文建模，每个决策基于前期部分计划构建，最终以系统级效用评估完整计划。在叮咚买菜和京东的真实运营数据上验证，SCOPE一致优于分阶段独立优化方法和实务基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链中，完整的补货计划包含品类选择、货源分配、补货频率和配送路线等决策，这些决策在运营上高度耦合（品类影响需求与负载、货源与频率重塑配送请求、路线可行性决定前期选择的价值），但在实践中往往由不同部门通过独立系统分别优化，导致缺货、库存风险和不必要的运输成本。

Method: SCOPE是一种复合策略模型，核心设计包括：(1) 将供应链实体（如品类、供应商、地点）表示为token；(2) 通过共享的运营表征对token进行上下文建模，捕捉实体间耦合关系；(3) 将每种token类型映射到对应的决策接口；每个决策基于前面决策形成的部分计划构建，最终完整计划通过共享的系统级效用函数进行评估。

Result: 在叮咚买菜（前置仓/末端补货）和京东（上游补货）两个不同补货层级的大规模供应链真实运营数据上，SCOPE在两类场景中均一致优于逐阶段独立优化的方法以及供应链运营中常用的实务基线，验证了跨部门运营耦合格的学习与协调能带来更有效的端到端供应链决策。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，相较于传统的分阶段独立优化，通过学习并协调跨部门运营决策之间的耦合关系，可以实现更有效的端到端供应链决策，为供应链AI从孤立决策模块迈向统一运营规划提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Can supply-chain AI move beyond isolated decision modules toward unified operational planning? A complete replenishment plan specifies which products each location carries, which upstream facility supplies it, how often it is replenished, and how deliveries are routed. These decisions are operationally coupled: the selected assortment changes the demand and load passed to later stages; source assignment and replenishment frequency reshape the delivery requests; and route feasibility and cost, in turn, determine the system value of the earlier choices. Yet in modern supply chains, these decisions are often handled by separate departments and optimized through separate systems, which can lead to stockouts, inventory exposure, and avoidable transportation. We propose SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination, a composite policy model that represents supply-chain entities as tokens, contextualizes them through a shared operational representation, and maps each token type to the corresponding decision interface. Each decision builds on the partial plan formed by earlier decisions while the completed plan is evaluated using a shared system-level utility. We instantiate this framework in urban fresh-retail replenishment, where service frequency, assortment, capacity pressure, and road-network routing interact strongly, and evaluate it on real operational data from Dingdong and JD.com, two large-scale supply chains operating at different replenishment echelons. Across both settings, SCOPE consistently outperforms methods that optimize each decision stage separately, as well as practice-oriented baselines commonly used in supply-chain operations. These results show that learning and coordinating cross-department operational couplings lead to more effective end-to-end supply-chain decisions.

</details>


### [180] [InfoOps Bench: A live information operations safety benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28503)
*Dorian Quelle,Lisa-Maria Neudert,Jonathan Bright,John Gallacher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个动态更新、持续运行的AI基准测试（InfoOpsBench），用于评估前沿语言模型被国家支持的信息操作所利用的风险。基于对2,100多个俄、中、伊国家支持信息操作的实时监控，测试了8家提供商的17个模型，发现大多数模型可被用于信息操作，诚信分数从8.8%到94.5%不等，且模型选择会影响操作的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家支持的信息操作是威胁前沿语言模型完整性和安全性的严重风险，而现有静态基准容易被模型记忆和饱和，缺乏对动态信息操作的持续评估。

Method: 从实时监控管道中提取超过2,100个国家支持的信息操作，构建动态基准测试；对来自8家提供商的17个模型，在四种提示框架下进行测试；定义"诚信分数"为拒绝请求的百分比，并分析模型规模、来源国、事实核查率等因素与合规率的关系。

Result: 大多数模型可被诱导用于信息操作；诚信分数跨度达85.7个百分点（8.8%-94.5%），且与模型规模无关；不同模型在信息操作中表现出不同特征（编造细节、消解声明、事实核查率2.9%-72.9%不等）；中国开发的模型在涉及中国批评的事实性声明上合规率骤降48-70个百分点。

Conclusion: 诚信对抗信息操作至少部分与模型对良性内容的拒绝倾向相关，这凸显了在模型可用性与安全性之间平衡的挑战；动态基准的持续更新特性使其免受饱和影响，为持续监控前沿模型完整性与信息操作抵御能力提供了工具。

Abstract: In this paper we present an active, constantly updated AI benchmark which measures the integrity of frontier language models against being co-opted for state-backed information operations. We draw on over 2,100 information operations from a live monitoring pipeline which tracks Russian, Chinese and Iranian state-backed information assets. Alongside this paper, we release a companion website that tracks the most prominent claims spread by state-backed media outlets, updated weekly, available from: pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench. The dynamic nature of the benchmark makes it resistant to saturation.
  In the benchmark, we test 17 models from 8 providers across four prompt framings. We find that most models can be co-opted for information operations. Integrity scores, defined as the percentage of refused requests, range from 8.8% to 94.5%, an 85.7-percentage-point spread not explained by model size. Model choice also changes the character of the resulting operation. Some models fabricate details and produce output more harmful than the source material, others defuse claims even while complying, and fact-checking rates vary from 2.9% to 72.9%.
  Integrity against information operations is at least partly related to refusal to produce content even for benign claims, illustrating the challenge of balancing model usability with safety. With one exception (Z.ai's GLM 5.2), the Chinese-developed models sharply cut compliance on factually grounded but China-critical claims, dropping 48-70 percentage points relative to matched benign claims.

</details>


### [181] [Selective Credibility-Limited Belief Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28523)
*Theofanis Aravanis,Costas D. Koutras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出选择性可信度受限信念更新框架，将认知输入相对每个源世界转换为更弱的代理后再执行可信度受限转移，支持复合认知输入的部分实现，并统一了标准的Katsuno-Mendelzon更新与可信度受限信念更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信度受限信念更新方法将认知输入视为不可分割的整体，无法表达仅部分复合认知输入可被实现的情形。

Method: 引入选择性可信度受限信念更新，通过将认知输入相对于每个源世界转换为较弱代理后再进行可信度受限转移；给出语义与公理化刻画，并识别两个良性子类：一致性保持更新算子与最大一致性保持更新算子。

Result: 提供了语义特征与公理系统；识别了两类良性子类（一致性保持与最大一致性保持）；证明可信度受限信念更新是特例，而Katsuno-Mendelzon更新在移除可信度限制且变换函数为恒等时恢复。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个统一且更具表达力的信念更新框架，涵盖既有方法并支持源相关的选择性接受。

Abstract: Belief update concerns changes in an agent's beliefs induced by changes in the underlying world. Standard Katsuno-Mendelzon update assumes that an epistemic input can be incorporated from every initially possible world, whereas credibility-limited belief update restricts, for each source world, the successor worlds regarded as credible or reachable. Nevertheless, existing credibility-limited approaches treat the epistemic input as an indivisible whole, and therefore cannot represent cases in which only part of a compound epistemic input can be realized. We introduce selective credibility-limited belief update, in which the epistemic input is transformed, relative to each source world, into a weaker proxy before the credibility-limited transition is performed. We provide semantic and axiomatic characterizations of the resulting class of update operators. We then identify two well-behaved sub-classes; namely, consistency-preserving update operators, which require every transformed epistemic input to be credible from its source world whenever the original epistemic input is consistent, and maximal consistency-preserving update operators, which additionally require the selected proxy to be maximally informative among the credible consequences of the original epistemic input. Finally, we establish the generality of the proposed framework by showing that credibility-limited belief update is recovered as a special case, while Katsuno--Mendelzon belief update emerges when credibility restrictions are removed and the transformation functions are taken to be identities. These results demonstrate that the framework provides a unified and strictly more expressive account of belief update, encompassing established approaches while supporting source-dependent selective acceptance.

</details>


### [182] [MANTA: Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation for Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28527)
*Mao-xun Huang,Jerry Wang,Yi-Cheng Lai,Zhengxin Zhang,Claire Cardie,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MANTA框架，使多智能体系统的通信拓扑在推理时自适应演化。在五个基准上取得了最高平均分74.0，超越最强基线5.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型多智能体系统通常将通信拓扑视为固定设计或离线优化目标，缺乏在推理时动态调整协作结构的能力，限制了系统在复杂任务中的适应性和性能上限。

Method: MANTA框架包含两个阶段：执行前，基于先验结构经验初始化任务条件的拓扑；部署时，监控协作轨迹并在当前组织结构不足时施加有界的结构更新，可修改智能体角色、通信链接、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径，同时保留任务接口和智能体预算。

Result: 在信息检索、工具使用、规划、工作流执行和数学推理五个基准上与单智能体和多智能体基线对比，MANTA取得最高平均分74.0，超越最强基线5.8个百分点，并在PlanCraft上获得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 推理时自我改进可以扩展到协作架构本身，通信结构不是静态约束而可以成为自适应优化的对象，为多智能体系统的架构自演化提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through task decomposition, agent specialization, information exchange, and intermediate validation. However, existing systems typically treat communication topology as a fixed design choice or an offline optimization target. We introduce MANTA, a framework for Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation that enables communication structures to self-evolve at inference time. Before execution, MANTA initializes a task-conditioned topology from prior structural experience. During deployment, it monitors collaboration traces and applies bounded structural updates when the current organization becomes insufficient. These updates can modify agent roles, communication links, execution order, information visibility, and validation pathways while preserving the task interface and agent budget. We evaluate MANTA against representative single-agent and multi-agent baselines on five benchmarks spanning information seeking, tool use, planning, workflow execution, and mathematical reasoning. MANTA achieves the highest average score of 74.0, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.8 percentage points and obtaining the best result on PlanCraft. These results show that inference-time self-improvement can extend to the architecture of collaboration itself.

</details>


### [183] [Rethinking Inference-Time Scaling in Local Computer-Use Agents: Failure Modes and Compute Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28573)
*Woongkyu Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究本地部署的计算机使用智能体（CUA）在严格硬件约束下的推理时缩放（inference-time scaling）效果，涵盖上下文、时间、结构和并行四个维度，发现在本地模型中额外计算往往收益递减，需要选择性计算分配和故障感知控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署CUA对隐私、成本和实用性日益重要，但严格硬件约束下如何提升其性能仍是未解难题。虽然推理时缩放已被证明能提升前沿CUA，但其对资源受限的本地模型是否有效缺乏系统研究。

Method: 在OSWorld基准上对Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B和OpenCUA-7B共四类本地模型进行系统实证研究，从上下文（contextual）、时间（temporal）、结构（structural）和并行（parallel）四个维度评估推理时缩放的影响。

Result: （1）上下文缩放能提供历史锚定，改善轨迹稳定性与任务准确率，但增益随token成本增加而饱和，且失败模式从重复/停滞轨迹转向过早的虚假成功；（2）时间缩放减少最大步数停滞，但未显著提升任务成功率，更长的时间视野往往延长错误轨迹而非纠正；（3）结构分解在本地两阶段智能体中引入规划与格式化开销；（4）并行缩放可部分缓解上述失败，但计算成本高昂。

Conclusion: 高效本地CUA需要选择性计算分配、故障感知控制机制，以及围绕本地模型能力与局限设计的智能体框架，而非简单增加计算资源。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous computer-use agents (CUAs) locally is increasingly important for privacy, cost efficiency, and practical usability, yet improving their performance under strict hardware constraints remains challenging. While recent studies show that inference-time scaling can improve frontier computer-use agents through additional computation during execution, its effectiveness for resource-constrained local models remains poorly understood. We present a systematic empirical study of inference-time scaling in local CUAs across contextual, temporal, structural, and parallel dimensions. We evaluate Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B, UI-TARS-1.5-7B, and OpenCUA-7B on the OSWorld benchmark. Our results show that additional computation often yields diminishing returns while changing failure modes. Contextual scaling provides historical grounding that improves trajectory stability and task accuracy, but its gains saturate as token cost increases and failures shift from repetitive or stalled trajectories toward premature false successes. Temporal scaling similarly reduces max-step stalls, yet does not substantially improve task success, indicating that longer horizons often extend erroneous trajectories rather than correct them. We further find that structural decomposition can introduce planning and formatting overhead in local two-stage agents, while parallel scaling partially mitigates these failures at a substantial computational cost. Overall, our findings suggest that efficient local CUAs require selective compute allocation, failure-aware control mechanisms, and agentic frameworks designed around the capabilities and limitations of local models.

</details>


### [184] [DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28580)
*Jiacheng Tao,Qingyun Sun,Haonan Yuan,Ziwei Zhang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DualG-MRAG，一种双层级框架，通过解耦的宏观推理图和微观匹配图来增强多模态检索增强生成（MM-RAG）在多跳推理任务上的表现，缓解细粒度视觉特征带来的图膨胀与检索噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MM-RAG方法在处理复杂多跳推理任务时存在局限：实例级匹配无法捕捉跨模态和跨文档的显式关系；图增强方法虽引入结构建模，但在多模态场景下面临两难——细粒度视觉特征导致图快速膨胀和检索噪声，粗粒度表征则丢失关键局部证据。

Method: DualG-MRAG采用双层级解耦架构：(1) 构建Macro Graph用于全局拓扑路由，Micro Graph用于精细局部验证，以隔离全局结构推理与细粒度证据匹配从而抑制检索噪声；(2) 将检索建模为查询驱动的消息传递过程，通过GNN检索器实现跨异构证据源的动态相关性传播；(3) 引入动态规划解码机制，直接从GNN前向传播中提取显式推理路径，替代孤立文档块作为生成模型的结构化输入。

Result: 大量实验表明DualG-MRAG在证据召回率和复杂问答准确性上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 解耦宏观推理与微观匹配的双层级图架构能够有效缓解多模态RAG中图膨胀与检索噪声之间的矛盾，显式推理路径提取为生成模型提供了连贯的结构化指导，从而显著提升复杂多跳推理性能。

Abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [FAVA: Formal Authorization for Verified Agents with Evidence-Backed Permission Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27267)
*Yifan Zhang,Xinkui Zhao,Sai Liu,Hengxuan Lou,Guanjie Cheng,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FAVA是一个基于权限携带的智能体授权框架，通过LLM引导的权限中间表示(IR)将自然语言任务转化为结构化约束，再通过确定性降低转换为带证据的权限图，最后用SMT求解器进行数学验证，在效果性动作执行前确保安全授权。在多个基准上的决策合规率达90.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态工具级权限不足以应对LLM智能体在动态环境中交替进行语义推理与复杂系统操作的场景，因为安全授权高度依赖上下文、运行时状态和数据流，需要一种上下文感知的正式授权机制。

Method: FAVA由三个核心组件构成：(1) LLM引导的Permission Intermediate Representation (IR)，将模糊的自然语言任务翻译为结构化约束；(2) 确定性降低通道，将IR转换为显式跟踪数据流、依赖关系和上下文标签的证据-backed权限图；(3) SMT求解器作者，在任何effectful动作执行前对当前图进行数学验证，并通过运行时网关强制执行求解结果（授权或携带精确反例拦截）。

Result: 在OpenAgentSafety、OctoBench和ActPlane三个场景中评估，FAVA在聚合数据集上达到90.5%的决策合规率(DCR)，并成功拦截了评测轨迹条件化场景中的动态违规轨迹。

Conclusion: FAVA展示了将LLM的自然语言理解能力与确定性形式化验证方法相结合的有效路径，为LLM智能体提供了一种上下文感知、证据驱动的正式安全授权机制，弥补了静态权限控制的不足。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents autonomously interleave semantic reasoning with complex system operations. In these dynamic environments, static tool-level permissions are fundamentally insufficient; safe authorization is highly context-dependent and heavily reliant on evolving runtime states and data flows. We present FAVA (Formal Authorization for Verified Agents), a permission-carrying authorization framework for agent execution. FAVA utilizes an LLM-guided Permission Intermediate Representation (IR) to translate ambiguous natural-language tasks into structured constraints. A deterministic lowering pass then converts this IR into an evidence-backed permission graph that explicitly tracks data flows, dependencies, and contextual labels. To provide strict security guarantees, a Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT) authorizer mathematically verifies the current graph against security policies before any effectful action executes. A runtime gateway then enforces the solver's result, either authorizing the execution or intercepting it with a precise counterexample. We evaluate FAVA across OpenAgentSafety, OctoBench, and ActPlane scenarios. Our evaluation demonstrates that FAVA achieves a 90.5% Decision Compliance Rate (DCR) over the aggregate dataset, successfully intercepting dynamic violating traces in the evaluated trace-conditioned scenarios.

</details>


### [186] [Open Security Benchmark: Towards Autonomous Enterprise Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27288)
*Gal Engelberg,Michael Arenzon,Leon Goldberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: OSB是一个用于评估企业自主网络防御中agentic AI的基准测试框架，通过冻结的企业安全状态快照和封闭式ground truth，在text-to-SQL和原生API两种模态下评估安全态势调查能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业正转向自主网络防御（agentic AI），但缺少一个公开、可查询、跨厂商的共享环境来端到端评估此类agent的可信度。现实企业环境是私有、跨厂商且深度关联的，导致"环境数据鸿沟"（environment data gap），领域内无法回答"agent是否值得被信任"这一核心问题。

Method: OSB由五个组件构成：数据层、任务与评估集层、多维评分层、最小可审计测试框架（harness）以及"自带环境"（bring-your-own）路径。它通过冻结企业环境为不可变快照，将答案锚定到封闭式ground truth，并在两种模态下评估态势调查：关系快照上的text-to-SQL和同一环境的服务实例上的各厂商原生API。框架用两个身份安全包和一组跨多规模的合成组织环境数据集进行实例化。

Result: OSB提供了可实例化的框架，已用两个身份安全任务包和多种规模的企业环境数据集验证，并规划了向更多态势子域、调查模态以及从评估到修复的防御阶段扩展的路径。该框架支持公开对比与私有租户评估共用同一基底。

Conclusion: OSB通过提供共享的、冻结的、封闭式的企业环境评估基底，填补了自主网络防御agent评估中的"环境数据鸿沟"，为领域内评估此类agent的可信度提供了标准化框架，并为未来扩展奠定了方向。

Abstract: Enterprises are moving toward autonomous cyber defense: agentic AI that builds situational awareness of an organization's security state and reasons from it to assessments, decisions, and actions. This rests on a holistic view of the enterprise's security state, the continuous, cross-vendor picture of identities, cloud and infrastructure, data, applications, and their configurations that security posture management assembles. As agents take on this work, what matters is not whether an agent can produce an answer but whether it should be trusted to. The field cannot yet answer this question. Real enterprise environments are private, cross-vendor, and deeply correlated, and none is exposed publicly as a shared, queryable target for evaluating such agents end to end. We call this the environment data gap. We present Open Security Benchmark (OSB), a framework that benchmarks agentic AI on this work. OSB surfaces a curated enterprise environment - a frozen, holistic view of the security state - and evaluates posture investigation across two modalities: text-to-SQL over a relational snapshot and each vendor's native API over a served instance of the same environment. Freezing the environment pins the target state as an immutable snapshot and anchors answers to a closed-form ground truth. OSB is built from five components: a data layer, a task and evaluation-set layer, a multi-dimensional scoring layer, a minimal auditable harness, and a bring-your-own path that serves public comparison and private tenant evaluation from one substrate. We instantiate the framework with two identity-security packs and a family of synthetic-organization environment datasets spanning multiple scales, and chart its extension to further posture subdomains, investigation modalities, and defense stages from assessment toward remediation.

</details>


### [187] [RoguePrompt: Dual-Layer Encoding for Self-Reconstruction to Circumvent LLM Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27373)
*Benyamin Tafreshian,Prathamesh Dhake*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RoguePrompt，一种将禁忌提示词分区并应用Vigenère和ROT13两层嵌套编码（配合自然语言重建指令）的越狱流水线，在黑盒威胁模型下对313个真实硬拒绝提示进行测试，结果显示过滤器绕过率93.93%、指令重建率79.02%、执行率70.18%，并提供了分阶段失败证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM越狱研究通常将可见接受、隐藏请求成功恢复和后续执行聚合为单一的"攻击成功"指标，导致缺乏关于多阶段提示转换攻击在黑盒交互中具体在哪个阶段失败的证据。作者旨在弥补这一空白，提供阶段级的失败定位分析。

Method: RoguePrompt流水线包含三个核心环节：(1) 将禁忌提示词进行分区；(2) 应用两层嵌套编码——先Vigenère后ROT13加密；(3) 附加自然语言重建指令使模型能够解码并执行原始请求。整个方法在黑盒威胁模型下（仅通过API或用户界面访问托管模型）开发和评估，测试集为313个真实世界硬拒绝提示，按"过滤绕过—指令重建—执行"三个阶段分别设定自动化评判标准来测量成功率。

Result: RoguePrompt平均达到93.93%的过滤器绕过率、79.02%的指令重建率以及70.18%的执行率。实验结果在证明分层提示编码有效性的同时，提供了阶段级别的证据，说明多阶段越狱在过滤绕过、指令重建和执行三个环节中各自的失败点。

Conclusion: 分层提示编码（Vigenère+ROT13配合自然语言重建指令）是一种有效的越狱方法，其阶段级评估方法为理解多阶段越狱攻击的失败机制提供了新的视角，有助于后续防御策略的针对性设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming increasingly integrated into mainstream development platforms and daily technological workflows, typically behind moderation and safety controls. Despite these controls, preventing prompt-based policy evasion remains challenging, and adversaries continue to "jailbreak" LLMs by crafting prompts that circumvent implemented safety mechanisms. Prior work has established cipher-mediated interaction, code-embedded decryption, prompt decomposition and reconstruction, and layered custom encryption as viable attack primitives. However, reported evaluations generally collapse visible acceptance, successful recovery of the concealed request, and subsequent execution into an aggregate attack-success outcome. This leaves limited evidence about where multistage prompt-transformation attacks fail within an observable black-box interaction. This paper introduces RoguePrompt, a jailbreak pipeline that partitions a forbidden prompt and applies two nested encodings, Vigenere followed by ROT13, along with natural-language reconstruction instructions. RoguePrompt was developed and evaluated under a black-box threat model, with only API or user-interface access to the hosted models, and was tested on 313 real-world, hard-rejected prompts. Success was measured in terms of moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution when the relevant stage exceeded its automated criterion. RoguePrompt achieved average rates of 93.93% for filter bypass, 79.02% for reconstruction, and 70.18% for execution. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of layered prompt encoding while providing stage-level evidence of where multistage jailbreaks fail during moderation bypass, instruction reconstruction, and execution.

</details>


### [188] [Low-Latency Bootstrapping for CKKS using Roots of Unity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27401)
*Jean-Sebastien Coron,Robin Koestler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SPRU（Sparse Roots of Unity）的CKKS同态加密方案新自举算法。与原始CKKS自举依赖多项式近似模约简不同，SPRU将模q的加法群直接嵌入复数单位根，从而在CKKS方案中原生求值，显著降低乘法深度，允许使用更小的环维度并提升效率。基于OpenFHE C++库的实现，在低槽数密文上实现了高达5倍的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始CKKS自举通过同态评估近似模约简的多项式来实现，这导致较大的乘法深度和计算开销，限制了自举效率。作者旨在探索一种更直接、更低深度的方法来执行自举，从而提升CKKS方案的实用性能。

Method: SPRU自举的核心思想是将模q的加法群同态嵌入复数单位根中，利用CKKS方案原生支持复数运算的特性，直接对单位根进行操作来完成模约简，从而避免了对高次近似多项式的依赖，降低了所需的乘法深度。

Result: 在OpenFHE C++库中实现后，SPRU自举在具有少量槽位的密文上实现了最高5倍的延迟降低，同时由于乘法深度降低，可以使用更小的环维度，进一步提升了整体效率。

Conclusion: SPRU自举通过将模约简嵌入单位根的方法，为CKKS方案提供了一种更高效的自举途径，特别适合低槽数密文的场景，显著改善了自举性能。

Abstract: We introduce Sparse Roots of Unity (SPRU) bootstrapping, a new bootstrapping algorithm for the CKKS homomorphic encryption scheme for approximate arithmetic. The original CKKS bootstrapping method relies on homomorphically evaluating a polynomial that approximates modular reduction modulo q. In contrast, SPRU bootstrapping directly embeds the additive group modulo q into the complex roots of unity, which can be evaluated natively in the CKKS scheme. This approach significantly reduces the multiplicative depth required for bootstrapping, enabling the use of a smaller ring dimension and improving efficiency. In practice, using the OpenFHE C++ library, SPRU bootstrapping achieves up to a 5x reduction in latency when applied to ciphertexts with a small number of slots.

</details>


### [189] [Granite: A Modular Methodology for Foundational Verification of Hardware-Software Leakage Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27480)
*Stella Lau,Andres Erbsen,Adam Chlipala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Granite是一种用于RTL处理器模块化验证的方法论，同时验证功能正确性和非泄漏性，通过泄漏感知的精化（基于确定性）将指令集架构（ISA）级泄漏契约与微架构级的逐周期执行建立基础性连接，并首次实现了模块化且基础性的ISA泄漏契约到线级观测的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器设计中存在时序侧信道泄漏问题，特别是通过已知和未知的时序侧信道泄露秘密信息。传统方法难以将ISA级的安全契约与微架构级的具体执行细节（如指令周期数、中断处理位置、子模块延迟等）系统地联系起来，缺乏模块化的验证方法。

Method: 核心技术是"泄漏感知的精化 via 确定性"（leakage-aware refinement via determinism），即建立正确性和机密性作为相对于一族周期级确定性规范机器的轨迹等价性。秘密无关的非确定性通过存在性参数化规范中的不可信确定性函数（仅作用于公开数据）来处理。子模块针对各自的泄漏感知规范进行证明，这些证明组合成全设计的保证。规格仅约束功能正确性和信息流依赖，不约束周期数、中断处理指令、子模块精确延迟。最终与认证的静态分析（识别加密常数时间代码）结合，消除所有中间规范（包括ISA契约本身）中的可信计算基。

Result: 证明了流水线RISC设计（具有猜测执行、精确中断和I/O）的逐周期时序完全由ISA泄漏契约中指定的可观测项决定。对于遵循加密常数时间纪律（保持可观测项独立于秘密）的程序，该结果排除了通过已知和未知时序侧信道的信息泄漏。证明了关于硬件-软件加密实现的逐周期机密性的单一Rocq定理。

Conclusion: Granite是首个实现指令集级泄漏契约与微架构级逐周期执行（含线级观测）之间模块化且基础性连接的工作，为安全处理器验证提供了完整的端到端验证链条，将所有中间规范从信任基中排除。

Abstract: Granite is a methodology for modular verification of both functional correctness and nonleakage of RTL processors against ISA contracts. We prove that the cycle-by-cycle timing of a pipelined RISC design--with speculation, precise interrupts, and I/O--is determined solely by observables specified in an ISA leakage contract. For programs that keep observables independent of secrets (i.e., following the cryptographic-constant-time discipline), this result rules out information leakage through known and unknown timing side channels.
  Granite's specifications only constrain functional correctness and information-flow dependencies: not how many cycles an instruction takes, at which instruction an interrupt is handled, or the exact latencies of submodules such as multipliers and memory. Granite's central technique is leakage-aware refinement via determinism, which establishes correctness and confidentiality together as trace equivalence with respect to a family of cycle-level, deterministic spec machines. Secret-independent nondeterminism is handled by existentially parameterizing specs with untrusted, deterministic functions acting only on public data. Submodules are proved against their own leakage-aware specs, and these proofs compose into the whole-design guarantee--which therefore holds over a space of secure implementations.
  We believe this work is the first to achieve modular and foundational connection between instruction-set-level leakage contracts and microarchitecture-specific cycle-by-cycle execution with wire-level observations. Our proofs compose with a certified static analysis that recognizes cryptographic-constant-time code to derive a single Rocq theorem about the cycle-by-cycle confidentiality of a hardware-and-software cryptographic implementation--eliminating every intermediate specification, including the ISA contract itself, from the trusted computing base.

</details>


### [190] [Flock: Fast Proving for Batch Boolean Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27491)
*Benedikt Bünz,Ron D. Rothblum,William Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Flock是一种基于哈希的SNARK系统，通过优化lincheck和zerocheck协议，实现大规模批处理布尔电路（如SHA-256、Keccak、BLAKE3）的极速证明。单核M4 Max上每秒可证明82k次BLAKE3压缩、42k次SHA-256压缩和30k次Keccak置换，开销低于原生的250倍；十核吞吐超过660k次BLAKE3压缩/秒，比Binius64快9倍以上，比最快的椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNARK应用的一个关键瓶颈是证明大批量标准密码学哈希求值（如SHA-256、Keccak、BLAKE3）。需要一种能够极速证明此类批处理布尔计算的方法。

Method: Flock结合了lincheck和zerocheck协议的新优化，并采用由编码代理协同设计的激进优化原型实现。它证明同一R1CS电路的批次（加上它们之间的输入/输出关系），支持哈希链和Merkle路径开证明，原则上可扩展为完整哈希签名验证。

Result: 单核M4 Max上每秒证明82k次BLAKE3压缩、42k次SHA-256压缩、30k次Keccak置换，开销为原生执行不到250倍；十核吞吐超过660k次BLAKE3压缩/秒。SHA-256证明比Binius64快9倍以上，比最快的椭圆曲线SNARK快500倍以上。

Conclusion: Flock在批处理哈希计算的SNARK证明中实现了数量级的性能提升，树立了新的state-of-the-art标杆，展示了布尔电路SNARK在实际大规模哈希证明场景中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: For many applications of SNARKs, a key bottleneck is proving large batches of standard cryptographic hash evaluations, such as SHA-256, Keccak, or BLAKE3. We introduce Flock, a hash-based SNARK for extremely fast proving of such batched Boolean computations. Flock proves batches of the same R1CS circuit (plus input/output relations between them), can prove hash-chains and Merkle path openings, and in principle can be extended to full-fledged hash-based signature verification. At its core, Flock combines new optimizations for the lincheck and zerocheck protocols with an aggressively optimized proof-of-concept implementation co-designed by coding agents.
  On a single core of an M4 Max processor, Flock proves 82k evaluations of the BLAKE3 compression function, 42k SHA-256 compressions, and 30k Keccak permutations per second --- less than a $250\times$ overhead over native execution. On ten cores, throughput exceeds 660k BLAKE3 compressions per second; in proving SHA-256, Flock is more than $9\times$ faster than Binius64, the prior state of the art, and more than $500\times$ faster than the fastest elliptic curve-based SNARK we measured against.

</details>


### [191] [Send and Pretend: Exploiting Transcript Consistency Issues in End-to-End Encrypted Group Chats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27510)
*Gabriel K. Gegenhuber,Moritz Grefner,Maximilian Günther,Matthäus Wininger,David Schmidt,Aljosha Judmayer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地证明了当前主流端到端加密（E2EE）即时通讯应用（如Signal、iMessage、WhatsApp、Threema）在群聊中无法保证转录一致性（TC），恶意群成员可以通过多种方式选择性省略、重排或篡改内容而不会触发任何UI警告，并提出了具体的利用场景（如操纵投票）和低开销的缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: E2EE消息应用被广泛用于群聊中的敏感协调（如政治决策），近期Signal和iMessage引入了投票功能，隐含假设所有参与者看到相同的对话视图（即转录一致性，TC）。然而现有协议是否真正保证该属性尚不清楚，本文旨在系统性验证这一问题。

Method: 作者在恶意参与者威胁模型下，系统地调查了主要E2EE信使（Signal、iMessage、WhatsApp、Threema）的群聊协议，识别了多个等价性（equivocation）向量，涵盖协议回退路径和群内成对投递通道的故意利用；并设计了具体攻击场景（社交工程、逃避审核、操纵投票）来验证这些向量，同时揭示了实现层面的隐私问题（如设备OS指纹识别）。

Result: 研究发现所有主流E2EE信使在群聊中都不提供任何形式的转录一致性保证，恶意群成员可以向不同接收者呈现不同的对话内容而不触发警告。研究发现了多个攻击向量并演示了具体利用场景，包括操纵投票结果和跨设备不一致投递，此外还发现了实现特有的隐私问题。

Conclusion: 转录一致性是当前E2EE群聊协议中被忽视的安全属性。作者将发现置于既有TC研究背景下，并提出了可集成到现有E2EE群协议中的低开销缓解措施和UI信号策略，以帮助检测和缓解这类攻击。

Abstract: End-to-end encrypted (E2EE) messaging apps are widely praised for their security and thus also used for sensitive coordination in group chats (e.g., by political decision makers). After Threema and WhatsApp, also Signal and iMessage have recently introduced polls to aid agreement processes in groups. This implicitly sets the expectation that all participants see the same outcome and thus have the same view of the conversation. This property is commonly referred to as transcript consistency (TC). In this work, we demonstrate that today's major E2EE messengers do not guarantee any form of TC for group chats, allowing a malicious group member to selectively omit, reorder, or present altered content to different recipients without triggering warnings in their user interface. We systematically investigate the extent of the problem under a malicious-participant threat model that targets the integrity of the shared transcript, or inconsistent delivery across a user's linked devices. We identify multiple equivocation vectors that range from protocol fallback paths to deliberate use of pairwise delivery channels within groups. We demonstrate concrete exploitation scenarios such as social engineering, evading moderation, and, in particular, rigging polls. Beyond these cross-service design issues, we also uncover implementation-specific behaviors with privacy implications (e.g., device OS fingerprinting). Finally, we contextualize our findings within prior transcript-consistency research and outline practical low-overhead mitigations and UI signaling strategies that can be integrated into state-of-the-art E2EE group protocols.

</details>


### [192] [ThreatForest: Multi-Agent Attack Tree Generation with Pluggable TTP Framework Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27528)
*Cristian Leo,Anton Dykyi,Danny Cortegaca,Daniel Begimher,Prakash Jha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ThreatForest是一个多智能体系统，可从源代码仓库自动生成结构化攻击树，并将其映射到MITRE ATT&CK、CAPEC等对抗性TTP框架，同时合成可操作的缓解措施。系统采用多阶段智能体管道（含人类验证点），在七个应用域上的威胁陈述、攻击树和缓解措施质量达到0.63-0.68（0-1量表），但发现嵌入编码器（而非多智能体管道）是TTP映射精度的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生架构的威胁建模依赖人工分析，速度慢且需要稀缺的安全专家资源。现有方法缺乏从代码仓库到TTP映射攻击树的端到端自动化能力，且缺少可复用的基准测试框架来评估此类系统。

Method: ThreatForest将威胁建模分解为多阶段智能体管道：仓库分析、上下文精炼、威胁生成、并行攻击树构建（含TTP映射与缓解合成）、报告生成。整个流程以有向图方式编排，包含确定性验证门、有界重试和三个人类参与验证点。使用领域特定的sentence-transformer通过余弦相似度将攻击步骤映射到候选TTP，并采用七域十六维度评分标准，由独立LLM评分器配合对抗性验证和专家评审进行评测。

Result: 威胁陈述、攻击树和缓解措施的质量分数为0.63-0.68，但仅依赖嵌入的TTP映射分数仅为0.29，且该差距在七个领域中保持稳定。对照实验表明，使用相同模型的单次调用基线使映射可辩护性提高一倍以上，证明瓶颈在于嵌入编码器而非多智能体设计。

Conclusion: ThreatForest是首个将代码仓库转换为TTP映射攻击树并提供基于证据的缓解措施、覆盖多个对抗性框架的端到端系统。研究贡献包括：一个完整的多智能体威胁建模系统、一个可复用的基准测试框架，以及一个关键发现——嵌入阶段的TTP映射是当前准确度的主导瓶颈，为未来优化指明了方向。

Abstract: Threat modeling is essential for secure software development, yet manual analysis of cloud-native architectures is slow and demands scarce security expertise. We present ThreatForest, a multi-agent system that generates structured attack trees from source code repositories, maps attack steps to adversary tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs) from a pluggable set of frameworks (MITRE ATT&CK, CAPEC, and cloud-specific threat matrices), and synthesizes actionable mitigations. ThreatForest decomposes threat modeling into a multi-stage agent pipeline -- repository analysis, context refinement, threat generation, parallel attack-tree construction with TTP mapping and mitigation synthesis, and report generation -- orchestrated as a directed graph with deterministic verification gates, bounded retries, and three human-in-the-loop validation points. A domain-specific sentence-transformer maps each attack step to candidate techniques by cosine similarity; we show empirically that this embedding stage, not the surrounding pipeline, is the dominant accuracy bottleneck. We evaluate ThreatForest across seven application domains on a sixteen-dimension rubric, scored by a panel of independent LLM raters with an adversarial verification pass and expert review. Panel-measured quality reaches 0.63-0.68 (on a 0-1 scale) for threat statements, attack trees, and mitigations, but only 0.29 for embedding-only TTP mapping -- a gap stable across all seven domains that isolates the binding constraint. A controlled single-call baseline on the same model more than doubles mapping defensibility, pinning the limitation on the embedding encoder rather than the multi-agent design. To our knowledge, ThreatForest is the first end-to-end system that turns a code repository into TTP-mapped attack trees with evidence-based mitigations across adversary frameworks, with a reusable framework for benchmarking such systems.

</details>


### [193] [AnchorMark: Robust Diffusion Watermarking via Latent-Space Rotation Synchrony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27551)
*Yuqi Qian,Yun Cao,Haocheng Fu,Haochen Zhao,Hong Zhang,Meineng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AnchorMark，一种无需训练、基于反转的鲁棒图像水印方法。利用"旋转同步性"属性——图像域旋转与恢复的初始潜变量中的旋转共享相同角度——在初始潜变量中心区域嵌入同步锚点，从而在提取时准确估计并校正旋转角度，显著提升在旋转及组合攻击下的比特准确性，同时保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反转的图像水印方法在复合有损后处理下仍然脆弱，尤其是涉及旋转时，旋转会破坏潜变量空间解码所需的空域对应关系，导致水印提取失败。因此需要一种对旋转鲁棒的训练-free水印方案。

Method: 首先发现"旋转同步性"这一潜变量空间属性：图像域的旋转与恢复的初始潜变量中的旋转角度一致。基于此，AnchorMark在初始潜变量的中心区域嵌入一个同步锚点，提取阶段通过该锚点精确估计旋转角度并进行校正，从而在旋转攻击下仍能正确解码水印。该方法无需额外训练，直接嵌入生成过程。

Result: 实验表明，AnchorMark在旋转攻击及组合攻击下显著提升了比特准确性（bit accuracy），同时对图像质量的影响很小。

Conclusion: 通过利用旋转同步性和同步锚点嵌入，AnchorMark有效解决了基于反转水印在旋转和复合后处理下的鲁棒性问题，是一种训练-free且保持图像保真度的鲁棒水印方案。

Abstract: Inversion-based watermarking embeds watermark payloads directly into the generative process, avoiding a separate post-hoc image-domain embedding stage while preserving the native visual fidelity of synthesized images. However, existing methods remain vulnerable to compound lossy post-processing, particularly when rotation is involved, as it disrupts the spatial correspondence required for latent-space decoding. To overcome this limitation, we introduce AnchorMark, a training-free, robust inversion-based watermarking. We uncover a latent-space property termed Rotation Synchrony: image-domain rotations and their counterparts in the recovered initial latent share the same angle. Building on this property, AnchorMark embeds a synchronization anchor in the central region of the initial latent, enabling accurate estimation and correction of the rotation angle during extraction. Experiments show that AnchorMark substantially improves bit accuracy under rotation and combined attacks, with limited impact on image quality.

</details>


### [194] [Revisiting the Adversarial Robustness of Graph-Based Traffic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27604)
*Qingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基于图的交通预测AI的鲁棒性评估，指出现有威胁模型不切实际、目标过于宽泛。作者提出一种物理感知的定向攻击（仅需有限的模型知识和少数传感器操纵），并引入一个物理信息检测器作为防御，将其输出作为输入特征喂给预测器，在自适应攻击下优于对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对交通预测的对抗鲁棒性研究使用不现实的威胁模型和不具针对性的目标，导致攻击和防御都需要重新审视。实际攻击者仅具备有限的模型知识，且能只对特定路段或路线进行局部化攻击（造成错误到达时间估计或不必要的重新导航），而这类场景尚未被充分探索；基于范数约束的对抗训练难以迁移到结构不同、物理感知的攻击。

Method: 1) 提出物理感知的定向攻击（physics-aware attack），模拟真实拥堵，仅操纵少数传感器，针对特定链路放大误差；2) 将鲁棒性重构为检测问题，引入学习到的物理信息检测器（physics-informed detector），其输出作为额外输入特征喂给加固的预测器；3) 在预测器固定的情况下，针对自适应攻击（adaptive attacks）进行训练。

Result: 物理感知攻击将目标链路误差放大数倍，而网络级误差几乎不变；针对范数约束扰动调优的对抗训练几乎无法抵御该攻击。所提出的检测-缓解防御在15个模型-数据集设置中的13个上，甚至优于直接针对物理感知攻击本身进行对抗训练的效果，在未见过的攻击上优势最大，且干净样本下的性能代价接近零。

Conclusion: 研究强调了需要在应用特定约束下审视抽象的AI对抗攻击，以评估其真实安全影响。物理感知的定向攻击和检测-缓解框架提供了更实用的鲁棒性评估与防御路径。

Abstract: Traffic forecasting by graph-based AI is a critical component of intelligent transportation systems, motivating security research on robustness to malicious sensor readings. We argue that prior robustness evaluations are largely shaped by unrealistic threat models and untargeted objectives, so both attacks and defenses must be revisited. We study a practical adversary with limited model knowledge and the ability to monitor and manipulate only a few road sensors. More importantly, practical attacks can be localized to specific links or routes, causing incorrect estimated arrival times or unnecessary rerouting while leaving the broader network largely unaffected. This targeted setting remains underexplored, and defenses such as adversarial training do not transfer well from the norm-bounded attacks they train on to structurally different, physics-aware attacks that mimic genuine congestion. We therefore reframe robustness as a detection problem, introducing a learned physics-informed detector whose output is fed to a hardened forecaster as an input feature and trained against adaptive attacks with the forecaster fixed. We evaluate across a variety of model architectures and benchmarks. The physics-aware attack multiplies target-link error several-fold while the network-wide error barely moves, and adversarial training, tuned to norm-bounded perturbations, barely dents it. Our detection--mitigation defense improves even on adversarial training hardened against the physics-aware attack itself, on $13$ of $15$ model--dataset settings and by the widest margin on a held-out attack, at near-zero clean cost. The results emphasize the need to examine abstracted AI adversarial attacks under application-specific constraints to assess their true security impacts.

</details>


### [195] [Strategy Phasing of Cyber Attacks on Digital Substations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27661)
*Akila Herath,Chen-Ching Liu,Junho Hong,Kuchan Park*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SubCASP，一种基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）的方法，用于融合入侵检测系统（IDS）的日志数据，推断IEC 61850数字化变电站网络攻击的当前阶段、下一阶段及回溯攻击路径，以支持主动缓解措施的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IEC 61850数字化变电站虽提升了运行效率，但攻击者可滥用其通信协议操纵断路器操作，造成严重系统影响。这类网络攻击基于多阶段策略构建，而现有IDS通常仅标记孤立的攻击症状，缺乏攻击阶段上下文，难以支持缓解措施的部署。

Method: SubCASP基于隐马尔可夫模型（HMM），融合IDS数据日志以推断攻击阶段。攻击阶段源自ATT&CK框架的威胁建模，模型在可复现的攻击图数据集上完成训练和评估。

Result: 测试结果展示了SubCASP在多种IDS可观测性水平和缺失IDS数据日志场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SubCASP通过HMM方法有效弥补了现有IDS缺乏攻击阶段上下文的不足，能够为变电站网络攻击的缓解措施部署提供阶段感知支持，并在数据不完整条件下保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Digital substations that comply with IEC 61850 have improved the operational efficiency of modern power systems. However, adversaries can abuse IEC 61850 communication to manipulate circuit breaker operations in substations, which can result in severe system impacts. These cyber attacks are crafted based on broader multi-phase strategies. The existing intrusion detection systems (IDSs) often flag only isolated symptoms. Thus, there is a lack of context in the attack phase to support the deployment of mitigation measures. This paper proposes Substation Cyber Attack Strategy Phasing (SubCASP), a Hidden Markov Model(HMM)- based method that fuses IDS data logs to infer the current attack phase, next attack phase, and retrospective attack path. The attack phases are derived from an ATT&CK-based threat modeling. The SubCASP model is trained and evaluated on a reproducible attack-graph dataset. Test results are presented to demonstrate the robustness of SubCASP for various IDS observability levels and missing IDS data logs scenarios.

</details>


### [196] [Distributed Point Functions and Function Secret Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27696)
*Elette Boyle,Niv Gilboa,Yuval Ishai,Peter Scholl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于分布式点函数（DPF）的综述文章，系统梳理了DPF的定义、构造及应用，并讨论了其向函数秘密共享（FSS）的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPF作为一种轻量级密码学原语，在隐私信息检索、匿名消息传递、安全计算预处理和伪随机相关生成器等领域有广泛应用，但缺乏系统性的梳理与总结，因此需要一篇综述性文章来整合已有成果。

Method: 作者对DPF的定义、密码构造方案及其各类应用进行了系统调查与分类整理，并进一步论述了从DPF到函数秘密共享（FSS）的推广，说明FSS如何将点函数泛化到更丰富的函数类，且高效的FSS方案能保持DPF大多数应用的类似推广。

Result: 文章汇总了DPF在隐私信息检索、匿名通信、预处理安全计算和伪随机相关生成器等领域的代表性构造与应用成果，并建立了DPF与FSS之间的关联框架。

Conclusion: DPF是功能强大且结构轻量的密码学原语，其向FSS的推广进一步扩展了应用范围，为隐私保护计算提供了重要的理论基础与实用工具。

Abstract: A distributed point function (DPF) is a cryptographic primitive that enables compressed additive sharing of a secret weight-1 vector (equivalently, a point function) across two or more parties. The appealing lightweight structure of DPF constructions has enabled a wide range of applications. These include private information retrieval, anonymous messaging, secure computation with preprocessing, and pseudorandom correlation generators for expanding small correlated seeds into large pseudorandom instances of cryptographic correlations.
  In this article, we survey definitions, constructions, and applications of DPFs. We also discuss the extension of DPF to function secret sharing (FSS), which generalizes point functions to support richer function classes. Efficient FSS schemes yield a similar generalization for most of the applications of DPFs.

</details>


### [197] [CHARGE: Leveraging CWE Hierarchies for Hardware Security SystemVerilog Assertion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27776)
*Xiao Tan,Cynthia Sturton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHARGE，一个利用CWE层次结构和大型语言模型自动为未验证RTL模块生成安全属性的框架，在Hack@DAC18/19/21设计上检测出27/42个已知漏洞，并在OpenPiton SoC中发现一个新漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RTL安全属性生成依赖可信设计规格，需要大量人工工程，而未经验证的RTL模块缺乏安全规格，导致安全验证困难。现有方法难以自动化识别安全关键资产并推断期望的安全行为。

Method: CHARGE框架利用CWE条目的层次结构特性，通过推理过程更准确地识别未验证RTL模块中的安全关键资产；随后从已识别资产和CWE语义中推断期望的安全行为，自动生成安全属性。使用OpenAI的GPT-4.1作为底层LLM。

Result: 在Hack@DAC18、19、21的SoC设计中，CHARGE检测出42个已知漏洞中的27个。在Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC上，89%的生成SVA在JasperGold FPV中可运行，92.2%非空洞。相比开源手动编写的属性，CHARGE正确为3个手动属性有误的漏洞编写了属性，并发现一个此前未识别的新漏洞。

Conclusion: CHARGE验证了结合CWE层次结构和LLM自动生成RTL安全属性的可行性，能够减少对可信规格的依赖和人工工程成本，并且能超越现有手动属性集发现新漏洞。

Abstract: This paper presents CHARGE, an automated framework for generating security properties for unverified RTL modules using CWEs and large language models (LLMs). The hallmark is a reasoning process that leverages the hierarchical nature of CWE entries to improve accuracy when identifying security-critical assets in unverified RTL modules. As a result, the approach can infer expected security behaviors and generate properties from identified assets and CWE semantics, avoiding the need for trusted design specifications and reducing manual engineering effort. We evaluate the framework on the Hack@DAC18, 19, and 21 open source SoC designs using OpenAI's GPT-4.1. CHARGE detects 27 of 42 known bugs in these designs. For Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC, 89% of the generated SVAs run in Cadence JasperGold FPV, and 92.2% are non-vacuous. We compare to an open-source, manually written set of properties for these designs and find that CHARGE correctly writes properties for three bugs in which the manually written properties were incorrect. In addition, CHARGE-generated properties identify a new bug in the Hack@DAC21 OpenPiton SoC that was not previously identified.

</details>


### [198] [Adaptive Security at the Edge for 6G-Enabled Healthcare IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27858)
*Ijaz Ahmad,Ijaz Ahmad,Erkki Harjula*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NANOEDGEGUARD是一个面向医疗物联网边缘网关的内核平面闭环速率控制机制，通过带滞回的流量控制钩子强制执行多级速率策略，相比用户空间防火墙基线可将99分位报警RTT降低13.3%，并减少46%的超额突发流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网服务依赖边缘网关转发日常遥测数据和罕见的时效性报警消息，即使短暂流量突发也会放大最坏情况延迟并干扰紧急消息，现有静态防火墙规则和用户空间控制环路响应不够及时。

Method: 设计了一个内核平面的闭环控制器，按源观测边缘流量强度，使用内核流量控制钩子强制执行可审计的多级速率策略，通过迟滞机制实现快速动作与显式恢复，并记录策略转换以供审计。

Result: 在树莓派网关上承载MQTT代理和两个ESP32端点（生成生命体征、报警和定时突发流量）的实验中，自适应内核平面速率控制相比用户空间防火墙基线将99分位报警RTT降低13.3%，同时维持与无强制相当的水平；相比无强制方案，超额放行的突发流量减少46%。

Conclusion: 自适应、可审计的边缘网关速率强制能够提升医疗物联网的韧性，并可在未来边缘智能中扩展为按需策略部署。

Abstract: Healthcare IoT services increasingly rely on edge gateways to relay routine telemetry and deliver rare but timecritical alarms. Even short traffic bursts can inflate worstcase delay and interfere with urgent messages. We present NANOEDGEGUARD, a kernel-plane closed-loop controller that observes per-source traffic intensity at the edge and enforces an auditable, multi-tier rate policy using in-kernel traffic-control hooks. Unlike static firewall rules or user-space control loops, our design prioritizes fast actuation and explicit recovery through hysteresis, and it records policy transitions for auditability. Using a Raspberry Pi gateway hosting an MQTT broker and two ESP32 endpoints generating vitals, alarms, and a timed burst, we show that adaptive kernel-plane rate control reduces the 99th-percentile alarm RTT by 13.3% compared to a user-space firewall baseline while maintaining no-enforcement-level RTT, and it reduces excess admitted burst traffic by 46% compared to no enforcement. These early results indicate that adaptive, auditable enforcement at the gateway can improve resilience for healthcare IoT, and it can be extended toward on-demand policy deployment in future edge intelligence.

</details>


### [199] [Don't Trust the AI Ecosystem: Analyzing Privacy Leakage in Compromised Open-Source Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27886)
